TOP 30 SAD SONGS OF 70S ERA

1. The Journey of Life – Safar (1970)

The journey of life—what kind of journey is this?
No one understood it, no one knew it.
What kind of path is this? Everyone walks upon it, yet
No one understood it, no one knew it.

We gave much love to life,
We shall fulfill our love for death as well.
We came into this world crying, but
We shall leave this world laughing.
But where shall we go? Who has this news?
No one understood it, no one knew it.

There are lives that were never truly lived,
Whom death claimed before they could start living.
There are flowers that never even bloomed,
Whom the autumn consumed before they could blossom.
The sight is troubled; the healers have grown tired.
No one understood it, no one knew it.

2. If a Spark Ignites – Amar Prem (1971)

If a spark ignites, the monsoon rain extinguishes it,
But if the monsoon sets the fire, who can extinguish that?
If autumn ravages the garden, the spring makes it bloom,
But if one is ravaged during the spring, who can make them bloom?

Do not ask us how the temple of dreams was shattered,
This is not a matter of outsiders; this is a story of one's own.
If an enemy causes a wound, a friend comforts the heart,
But if the beloved inflicts a wound, who can heal that?

Who knows what would have happened, who knows what I would have done,
I drink, so I am alive; if I didn't, I would have died.
If the world keeps one thirsty, wine quenches that thirst,
But if wine creates the thirst, who can quench that?

I admit that before a storm, no one's power works,
It isn't the fault of the waves; it is the fault of someone else.
If the boat falters in midstream, the boatman steers it across,
But if the boatman sinks the boat, who can save it?

3. Somewhere Far Away When the Day Sets – Anand (1971)

Somewhere far away, when the day sets,
The bride of evening steals her body and arrives silently.
In the courtyard of my thoughts,
Someone lights the lamps of dreams.

Sometimes, just like that, when my breath falters,
When my eyes fill up just sitting there,
Then, with a restless stir of love,
Someone touches me, but remains invisible to my sight.

Somewhere, these hearts are never able to meet,
Somewhere, the bonds of many births emerge.
The confusion was dense; my poor heart,
Despite being mine, suffers the pain of others.

My heart knows all these deep secrets,
How my dreams have turned golden.
These dreams of mine—these are my only own,
Their shadows shall never part from me.

4. What Happened to Your Promise – Hum Kisise Kum Naheen (1977)

What happened to your promise, that vow, that intention?
The day my heart forgets you will be the last day of my life.

I remember you said:
"I will never be upset with you."
Our hands have met like our hearts,
"How can they ever be parted?"
Every evening spent in your arms—
O unfaithful one, do you not remember even this?

Those who call me a deceiver—
Tell me, who is the deceiver?
The one who took sorrow for the sake of love,
Or the one who sold love away?
What kind of intoxication of wealth is this,
That you remember nothing at all?

5. Someone Broke My Heart So – Amanush (1975)

Someone broke my heart in such a way,
Turned me toward ruin in such a way,
They left after turning a decent man into a beast (Amanush).

The ocean is so close to me,
Yet there is a thirst in my life.
The thirst is great, but life is short.

The paths of this world say:
"There is no destination for you."
They bonded me with failures.

A setting sun rises again,
Darkness does not remain forever.
But my sun was so upset,
That I never saw the morning.
Even the lights have abandoned me.

6. This World, This Gathering – Heer Raanjha (1970)

This world, this gathering, is of no use to me.

To whom should I tell the state of my restless heart?
I am the extinguishing lamp of my own tomb.
I wish I could forget, but I cannot;
With what pomp did the funeral of my spring depart!

Neither can I find my own address, nor news of my beloved,
May even an enemy not receive such a punishment for love.
Those who seek God have found Him,
But I only seek a glimpse of my beloved.

Even in the desert, I found no refuge,
I found no excuse to forget my sorrow.
In a world where the heart craves for love—what should I think of such a world?
Having lost a winning game, I search for my lost friend.

Far from the eyes, someone sheds tears,
How can I not go? Someone is calling me.
Either mend my broken heart or break all the bonds;
O mountain, give me way! O thorns, release your hold!

7. O Companion (Sad) – Muqaddar Ka Sikandar (1978)

O companion, what is living without you?

In the flowers, in the buds, in the alleys of dreams,
There is nothing anywhere without you.

In every heartbeat is the thirst for you,
In my breath is your fragrance.
From this earth to that sky, in my sight, it is only you.
May this love not break; may you not be upset with me;
May this companionship never end.

Without you, my nights are like a hermit; without you, my days are nomads.
My life is like burning droplets; all my dreams are extinguished.
Without you, my life—and without me, yours—
This life is not truly life.

8. The Name Will Fade Away – Kinara (1977)

The name will fade away, this face will change,
My voice is my identity, if you remember.

The cruelties of time are not few;
Today we are here, who knows where tomorrow?
If we meet somewhere beyond the reach of time...
My voice is my identity.

That which passed was a matter of yesterday,
It wasn't a lifetime, just a hope.
If the reward for the night is found again somewhere...
My voice is my identity.

Where the day sets and the night is near,
Keep the path of life high as you walk.
If you ever remember and your heart feels sad...
My voice is my identity.

9. There is No Complaint from Life Without You – Aandhi (1975)

Without you, there is no complaint from life,
But without you, even life is not quite life.

I wish it were so—that from your footsteps,
The destination would pick itself up and go far away.
If you are with me, there is no lack of destinations.

The heart feels like, in the shelter of your garment,
I should keep my head bowed and keep crying.
Is there no moisture of tears in your eyes too?

If you say so, then tonight
The moon will not set; stop the night.
It is a matter of the night, and life doesn't remain forever.

10. I Am a Disgraced Poet – Namak Haraam (1973)

I am a disgraced poet—what else should I say?
Look, I have arrived—what should I say?

I, the disgraced poet—why did I come here?
I don't know why I left, I don't know what happened;
Something happened such that everything happened.

I have left you this gift,
Why I came here, I did not know, my love;
But it has happened... I am a disgraced poet.

That city I found, this city I lost;
By meeting someone, did I find someone or not?
That person is lost, who was mine, but...
That own one is lost, who was mine.

11. Having Broken My Heart into Pieces – Dada (1979)

Having broken my heart into pieces, I walked away with a smile.
There is nothing but pain, yet I still give you my blessings.

How restless I was, how troubled I was,
I didn't think of this, nor did I know that.
They came after making me pine, but
I would have come, but I found myself unable to go.

Because you desired it, I kept on loving;
I didn't think of this, nor did I know that.
I just kept on loving you just like that;
I kept receiving it, you kept receiving it.

12. The Night is Passing Away – Amar Prem (1971)

The night is passing away, the night is passing away,
But Shyam (the beloved) has not come.

Sleep does not come to the eyes,
Nor does he leave my dreams.
How should I say? What should I tell them?
My eyes met his—then why did they sleep?

Awakening hope in the heart,
Lighting lamps upon the eyelids,
Of his coming or his going—
No one brings any news.

Going to the riverbank,
I find myself unable to go.
Who can tell? Who can make me hear?
Whom should I call my own?

13. In Your Eyes, Something... (Sad) – Ghar (1978)

In your eyes, something... means yes,
They have told me
That you love me, my love.

I have found you, I have found you,
But I was lost as soon as I found you.
I didn't touch you, but if you said yes, then yes.

Whatever was connected to you,
That will remain forever.

14. How Things Have Changed – Guide (1965)

How things have changed in the blink of an eye,
You weren't unfaithful, yet you became unfaithful.

No other night like this will come again,
And there is no promise of meeting, but
The heart must not have given up the desire to meet.
I will leave you, but
That gaze has already taken over my heart.

The heart broke and she began to cry like this;
Even though the weather was lovely and spring had arrived,
Like moonlight hidden in a cloud,
She emerged but drifted far away.

15. Your Eyes are Full of Life – Safar (1970)

Your eyes are full of life—why have they filled with tears?
That which I could not say, those eyes have spoken.

It is a moment of pain, there is moisture in the eyes,
Yet why does this heart tremble?
What should I do? What should I say?
How can I explain it to anyone?
That which is in the heartbeat has come into the eyes.

That which you saw, that which you found,
This is the memento of that moment.
Why has it become a stranger—
This story of moments passed?
Every gesture has spoken this truth.

16. This Night of Our Meeting – Abhimaan (1973)

This night of our meeting—
Lest it get lost somewhere in pride.

I have considered it mine so much,
That I have no sense of my own self.
Tonight is not a night, the evening is not pleasant;
Lest both of us get lost somewhere.

You are the ocean, I am the river,
We met, changing like this.
From two directions, the monsoon says:
"Water and only water"—may it be so between us
That no distinction remains.

Without you, this monsoon is lonely;
Without you, even my own self
Seems forgotten to me.
In such solitude, both of us
Could not endure even for two moments.

17. A Song of Love (Sad) – Shor (1972)

It is a song of love, the flow of the waves;
Life is nothing else but the story of you and me.

One has to lose after gaining, one has to gain after losing;
The meaning of life is simply coming and going.
From a life of two moments, we have to steal a lifetime.

The storm has to come and pass away after coming;
This is a cloud of a few moments, it has to pass after spreading.
Shadows remain, memories remain as a sign.

18. Promise You Won't Leave – Aa Gale Lag Jaa (1973)

Promise that you won't leave,
That you won't leave my side.

No matter what anyone says, no matter if they don't agree,
Consider me as your own.
Tell me something now,
For there is no peace without you.

You have to come one day, after making me your own;
I have to live here now, just like this for you.
We are here at every moment, so why aren't you here too?
If I am with you, there is peace;
Without you, there is no living here.

19. What is This That Happened – Amar Prem (1971)

What is this that happened?
I don't know what has happened to me.

Somewhere, the heart has considered someone as its own,
That is why every moment feels like a crazy atmosphere.

Today my sight is trapped in someone's tresses,
There is someone who resides in my thoughts.

Why did he descend into the eyes as a dream?
I am in love—why did this realization come to the heart?

20. I Was Never Unfaithful – Shalimar (1978)

I was never unfaithful; I am the same even today.
If you had known the news of your becoming unfaithful...
I was never unfaithful.

I used to meet you, but
The day we were meant to meet was today.
You took it as a joke, but I gave you my heart.

Why do these tears cry in the eyes?
Dreams come in sleep.
I have found nothing but sorrow, even in the flowers now.

21. How Much I Love You (Sad) – Kudrat (1981)

How much I love you, I do not know,
But I cannot live without you.

If you are with me, then what have I not found in the world?
When you come close, it feels as if everything has arrived.
Why this heart beats, I shall not tell you.

In the lines of these hands, someone's name was written;
As soon as your name arrived, my own address was written.
Let someone explain to you that my living is because of you,
That I am yours.

22. My Drenched Eyes – Anuraag (1972)

Descend from my drenched eyes, you;
This water of the night—who knows where it will take me?

In our eyes, this night is fragrant;
In our arms, this cloud is flowing.
But there is no such matter that does not give a message of love.

You called out, and I don't know why I came too;
Two shadows met in solitude, to get lost in solitude.
Let us descend into each other's hearts and see what lies within.

23. O Boatman – Khushboo (1975)

O boatman, find your own shore.

The cage is broken; I have flown away.
Nothing could stop me; wings were not needed.
The cage is broken.

I am flying toward the clouds,
Then why does the thread pull at my heart and body?

Who knows what kind of thread this is,
That binds me even without being tied?

24. The Heart Searches – Mausam (1975)

The heart searches for those same days and nights of leisure.

Who knows where she went?
A forgotten story—looking eye to eye;
Why has life become so silent?

Who was it who once shared joys with us,
And then got lost in the crowd of sorrows?
No one lets me go.

25. People Will Say Something – Amar Prem (1971)

People will say something; it is the job of people to talk.
Leave these useless things, lest the night passes away in them.

Why have we defamed love time and again?
If we weren't meant to love, then why did we love?
You are unfaithful, and I am not the same either.

If you start forgetting your memories today,
At every step, you will start leading me astray.
Tell me, what do you want to do?

26. I Am a Poet of a Moment or Two – Kabhi Kabhie (1976)

I am a poet of a moment or two,
My story is of a moment or two.
My existence is of a moment or two,
My youth is of a moment or two.

So many poets came before me and departed;
Some left as soon as they arrived.

Whatever was their duty, they took it with them;
There were other duties too, which I could not understand.
They remained—and that fact remains even today.

A poet has no [other] purpose in the world;
Yes, a poet also has a desire.
Every single moment is a song; every single moment is a story.

27. If Only There Was Someone of My Own – Mere Apne (1971)

If only there was someone of my own, whom I could call my own.
Walking alone in these lonely paths...

People meet, and then they part,
Each takes their own path.

"No one stays with you for life"—everyone says this.
I too accepted this, but I still say:
If only there was someone of my own...

28. The Monsoon Falls in Droplets (Sad) – Manzil (1979)

The monsoon falls in droplets, the mind smolders.
In the drenched nights, your memory torments the mind.

Darkness has spread; the lonely mind is surrounded.
There is no such matter; there is no wakeful night;
Why does my peace leave me?

Only things about you from the heart, these sounds on my lips;
Taking these messages, I go from my own self.

29. In Your Alleys – Hawas (1974)

I shall not be in your alleys tomorrow, even if I cannot remain.
When I break away and leave, you will remember me.

After parting from me, such days will also come to you,
When my sorrow will be remembered by you very, much.

You will know the season of autumn, not through flowers,
When one by one, all your companions will reject you.

There was a day when I pined to meet you;
This is the day when even after meeting you, we shall not be able to meet.

30. In the Journey of Life – Aap Ki Kasam (1974)

In the journey of life, such turns will come,
Which, once passed, will never return.
Even if we meet, we shall not be able to meet.

On the path, you will meet some people
Who will give you company, but will not fulfill the companionship.

A starlit night will come before you,
And counting the stars, we will get lost in the wait.

TOP 30 ROMANTIC SONGS OF 80S ERA

1. How Much I Love You – Kudrat (1981)

How much I love you, I do not know,
But I cannot live without you.

I’ve heard that people endure the sorrow of separation,
I wonder how people spend their lives [alone].
Even a single day here feels like a year;
How much I wait for you, I do not know.

If someone else looks at you, my heart burns;
With great difficulty, my heart then composes itself.
What efforts I make—what do you know of them?
How restless this heart is, I do not know.

2. Between You and Me – Ek Duuje Ke Liye (1981)

Between you and me, what kind of unknown bond is this?
I didn't know it, you didn't know it.

One end of the thread is pulled, and the other comes running;
It comes bound by a fragile thread,
Just like someone who is madly in love.

I will wear the bangles from your hands,
My palanquin will go to your courtyard.
No matter what the world does [to stop us].

Friends speak many languages,
But in this world, love has only one tongue—
The one spoken by the candle and the moth.

3. I Saw a Dream – Silsila (1981)

I saw a dream, and so these sequences began;
As far as the eyes can see, flowers are blooming.
There is a complaint against your eyes—
Even if a flower is between us, it feels like a distance.

Your fragrance resides in my breath,
This is the magic of your love.
Your voice is in the winds,
The color of love is in the atmosphere.
Your songs are mingled in my heartbeats;
What should I say? My lips are sealed with shyness.

My heart is in your shelter,
Come, let me hide you in my arms.
Your image is in my eyes,
There is light on the paths for a long way.
Even if the caravans of light do not come tomorrow,
Thousands of lamps of love are already lit.

4. I Took Your Name – Ram Lakhan (1989)

(F): I have so many complaints against you,
You’ve met me after so many days;
Tell me, what did you do all these days?
(M): I took your name, I remembered you.

(F): Did you talk to my mummy? (M): No.
(F): Did you meet my daddy? (M): No.
(F): Did you play any tricks on me? (M): No, never.
(F): Then what else did you do?
(M): I took your name, I remembered you.

(F): Did you get movie tickets? (M): No.
(F): Did you bring any good news? (M): No.
(F): Were you of any use to me? What did you do in love?
(M): I took your name, I remembered you.

(F): When will I become a princess? (M): Right now.
(F): When will we get freedom? (M): Right now.
(F): When will our wedding happen? (M): Right now, let’s go!
(F): Oh God! (M): Why are you holding your heart now?

5. The Heart is Crazy – Maine Pyar Kiya (1989)

The heart is crazy, it won't listen without the beloved;
This is a mad thing, it won't understand by explaining.
It thumps and wanders here and there, day and night.

The world asks for its wishes; I only ask for my beloved.
May my beloved and his home remain safe;
Other than this, the heart asks nothing from God.

I wish to turn myself into a veil and wrap you around my body,
Sometimes I think I should fly and take you to the sky.
The heart has other intentions too—what can I say?

6. At the Seashore – Saagar (1985)

At the seashore, this heart calls out:
If you are not here, I have no one.

The sights have awakened, the winds have awakened;
When love awakened, the atmospheres awakened.
Oh, for a moment,
The world of the heart has not slept.

Fairies of light dance upon the waves,
I am lost, like rivers in the ocean.
Oh, you are not the only one who is lost.

7. Chandni, O My Chandni – Chandni (1989)

Chandni, O my Chandni, you are my moonlight.

With the colorful clouds, with the kohl of your eyes,
I have written your name upon this heart.

(F): You did this work without asking; you’ve made me infamous.
Well, leave what people say;
If you love me, then what are you afraid of?
Don't wipe the words written on the heart with your veil.

(F): Why don't you ever bring a flower or a gift?
You are grown up; should I explain to you, should I teach you love?
One shouldn't come to meet their beloved empty-handed.
(M): Today I forgot again; I will remember this from tomorrow.

(M): By writing my name on your heart, you’ve just made a point;
What do you know? I have placed your picture inside my heart.
In love, you have won against your mad lover.

8. You are the One – Yeh Vaada Raha (1982)

You, you are the one whom my heart called its own.
Where you are, there I am;
Now, living without you is a punishment.
May we meet in such a way, like two waves meet
And never part again—this is the promise.

If I am the voice, you are my song;
My soulmate is unique from the whole world.

May this companionship never break at any turn,
May your hem never slip from my hand.
Even in a dream, may you never be upset with me;
May no one steal the joys of my love.

I shall steal you from the eyes of the world,
I shall hide you somewhere in a corner of my heart.
If difficulties ever arise in life,
You support me, and I shall support you.

9. You Who Are Colored in My Colors – Maine Pyar Kiya (1989)

You who are being colored in my colors,
Are you a fairy or the queen of fairies?
Or are you my love story?
Answer my questions, please answer.

Tell me, why do these moon and stars
Stare at your face so?
Why did the wind become fragrant after touching your body?
Why is the night so intoxicated?

Why are you so shy?
You seem a bit nervous.
Why is your veil slipping?
Why is there this stir in my heart?

An unnamed confusion on both sides,
As if a groom and bride have met.
Why is the condition of both like this?
After all, why is there so much love?

10. Where Have We Come – Silsila (1981)

Where have we come, just walking along together?

Only in your arms, my love, do my body and soul melt.

Is this the night, or is your hair flowing open?
Is this moonlight, or have my nights been washed by your gaze?
Is this the moon, or your bangle?
Are these stars, or your veil?
Is it a gust of wind, or the fragrance of your body?
Is this the rustling of leaves, or have you whispered something secretly?

You are the body, I am the shadow;
If you are not there, where am I?
O you who love me, where you are, I am there.
We were meant to meet, walking on this very path.

Every breath of mine is fragrant, like mild sandalwood;
Your love is moonlight, my heart is like a courtyard.
My evening has become even softer as it sets.

These circumstances are helpless, on both sides;
This night of solitude is here, and it is there.
There is much to say, but to whom should we say it?
How long should we remain silent and keep enduring?
The heart says we should abolish every custom of the world;
The wall that is between us—let’s tear it down today.
Why should we keep smoldering in our hearts? Let’s tell the people:
Yes, we are in love, we are in love, we are in love.

11. O My Companion – Qayamat Se Qayamat Tak (1988)

O my companion, where is this path?
Tell me the sign of where we shall meet.
My heart calls out to you—
For now we must pass through there,
That is where we must come, my love.

It is night, we are at the seashore;
If you come, then I too am defeated [by love].
This heart is restless, love too is restless;
For it is the season for meeting.

12. You Who are Smiling So Much – Arth (1982)

You, who are smiling so much—
What is the sorrow that you are hiding?

Why is there a touch of moisture in your gaze?
Why are you avoiding [eye contact]?

There is no complaint about anything;
This is a distance, a distance...
But is this a distance, or a harvest (fasla) [of deeds]?
This is a distance, not a harvest.

For whom has this home been plundered?
For God's sake, come now;
Lest it be too late,
Though it is already getting very late.

13. Our Love Will Remain Immortal – Muddat (1986)

Our love will remain immortal,
Whether it be victory or defeat.

Heartbeats became one when we met;
May this wind not change even in a changing world.
Tell the weather to pause for a moment.
Love has plundered us, yet we shall still love;
We will not mend our ways.

It was the moment of confession; we shall remember it.
What days those were when we met!
You wouldn't have come on the paths of love just like that;
If I had called you, you would have come.
Until now we were lonely;
You wouldn't have come, and I wouldn't have gone.

14. When We Are Young – Betaab (1983)

When we are young,
You will be a girl, the seasons will change;
Then we shall make you our own.

This youth, this era, this moment—
What can I say of the weather? It is for us to say.
Whenever we desire you, do not come then;
When I call you, then I will call for you.

You are as you are today, I am as I am today,
As if you were made for me,
As if I were made for you.
Tomorrow, when you are in the future, it won't be like this;
Wherever I may be, you will be there too.

15. Before My Eyes – Aashiqui (1990)

Before my eyes, close to my heart,
You are a wave that is flowing.

I wonder, if you are here, why is there solitude?
If the heart explains, only then will I accept it is a mistake.
The ink of mistakes, the story of punishments—
Has made me a reference for such a life.

Is this night beautiful, or is it your arrival?
For the magic of moonlight is not going to stop.
If I lose this night, tell me what is the matter;
Why is my life a light after you?

16. These Days and Nights Won't Pass – Mr. India (1987)

These days and nights cannot be passed,
Where is peace without you?
Come from afar, do come;
Come, come, my love.

The rays of the sun are thirsty for the earth,
The waves of the ocean are thirsty for the rivers.
You didn't know how thirsty these arms are.

We both used to laugh together;
Now that you are not here, what has happened?
I too am not [the same], I am lost within myself.
I am all alone—how should I say it?

17. The Day is Wondrous – Qayamat Se Qayamat Tak (1988)

The day is wondrous today—I found you.
Whatever happened, happened; now you and I have met.

Our eyes met, and something happened;
Why? I don't know why it happened.
Just like that, something happened and my heart beat;
Who knows why it beat?

In love, neither you nor I should be so afraid;
Yes, slowly comfort the heart.
Something happened and my heart became restless;
Who knows why it became restless?

You saw me, I saw you;
Then what happened? Only the crazy heart knows.
Something happened when our gazes met;
Who knows why they met?

18. I Don't Know Why After Meeting You – Pyaar Jhukta Nahin (1985)

I don't know why, after meeting you, this heart became restless;
It feels as if I have fallen in love.

What will you know of this city of desire?
You are unaware for now; you are a stranger.
I hadn't thought that we would meet like this;
I used to think that after meeting, we would never part.

I had thought I wouldn't love anymore;
When you came, I don't know why this heart became restless.
If you are not there, it feels as if nothing remains in the heart.

19. Someone Like You – Qurbani (1980)

If someone like you comes into my life, then it would be a success.
If you come, it would be a success.

O beloved, just look at me once;
The season of love has arrived, I’ve called you on an excuse.

At night, sleep does not come—why is it so thirsty?
In the wakeful nights, why is it so lost?
Look at the mirror—tell me, who is this?
Look toward me; it is you.
You are my desire.

Who knows what will happen now? The heart is broken, everything is upset.
If you are coming, then come; come into my arms.
In such a time, if you love me, what is the punishment?

20. Come, the Evening is Nearing – Maine Pyar Kiya (1989)

Come, the evening is nearing; come to me.

I remember you; sleep and peace depart.
It torments me, it bothers me—why do you test me?

Without you, my heart doesn't feel at home;
Talk to me, please talk to me.
Don't look like that, don't make it night [with your silence].

Why be afraid if there is nothing?
If there is a heartbeat, then why be afraid?
You are the one; then why be afraid?

21. My Companion – Qayamat Se Qayamat Tak (1988)

My companion, where is this path?
Tell me the sign of where we shall meet.
My heart calls out to you.

For now we must pass through there,
That is where we must come, my love.

It is night, we are at the seashore;
If you come, then I too am defeated [by love].
This heart is restless, love too is restless;
For it is the season for meeting.

22. What Can the Heart Do – Julie (1975)

What can the heart do when someone falls in love with someone?
Who should say it? What should this world say?

This heart doesn't stop anywhere; it doesn't listen to anyone.
It doesn't come into anyone's hand; it doesn't meet anyone.
What kind of thing is this heart? It is not understood.
One calls it "crazy."

Even in the season of happiness, it sheds these tears.
If it meets someone, it silently gets lost.
Finding someone, losing someone—this itself is called being crazy.
This is what they call love.

23. In Your Love – Tridev (1989)

In your love, what should I not do?
I would give my life; I would give my desires.

Wherever you look, you see my love;
It sways and torments—may you remain mine.

It is a dark night of sorrow; why should my heart not burn?
If you are not here, then what is there?
Heartbeats will stop, breaths will halt.
If you meet, I will live; I will become yours.

24. When Something Goes Wrong – Jurm (1990)

When something goes wrong, when a difficulty arises,
You must stay by my side, O companion.

There is no one, nor was there anyone in my life besides you.
When I found you, I found this whole world.

In the dark nights of sorrow, in the shadows of solitude,
When you are standing there, it feels like I’ve forgotten the sorrow of the world.

25. You are in My Heart – Satyamev Jayate (1987)

You are in my heart, you are in my eyes;
Every moment there is this realization.
Where is there peace until I meet you?

You looked at me, and you looked at me like that;
I hadn't thought I would think this.
What has happened? Have the eyes themselves been lost?
I found you only after being lost.
My words stop only at you.

I am sure we shall meet;
But even after meeting, why shall we meet like strangers?
You have a complaint against me—what do people say?
If you say so, I shall say—what is true and what is a lie?
If it is true, why shouldn't we say it? If it is a lie, then let’s say that.

26. A Vow to Make You Mine – Sadak (1991)

I have taken a vow to make you mine;
Finding you is the story of my life.

I am crazy for you; do love me.
Hold me in your arms; make me unaware [of the world].
My heartbeats have become yours;
Tell me, where is the sign of my existence?

I am crazy for you; do trust me.
Hold me in your arms; make me restless.
My soul too has a longing for you;
Whatever it be, tell me—it is not unknown.

27. O Maria – Saagar (1985)

O Maria, in the arms of the beloved.
Come, come, do come.

The heart is pining to meet you;
I don't know why, but I am sure
That you too will become mine and love me.
I admit we haven't met yet,
But I am sure we shall definitely meet one day.
When we meet...
Who knows what will happen then?
Just think—how things will change!

You are not here—then why is there this feeling
As if you are close to me?
By settling you in my heart, by decorating you in my sight,
I have already made you my own.
That dream of love I saw—
It has brought color [come true].
You too come;
Do come into my arms.

28. What Sorrow if I am Alone – Qayamat Se Qayamat Tak (1988)

What sorrow is there if I am alone?
As long as you have arrived.
What is the sorrow of the world’s grief?
As long as you have arrived.

If you are with me, even the darkness is pleasant.
If you are with me, why should I grieve for sorrows?
What is this night?
If you are here, every moment is spring.
If you are with me, what business do I have with the world?

I still remember that moment when you met me;
Was it pain or happiness? It is something.
When you met, it was happiness; when you didn't, it was sorrow.
But now that you are here, even the sorrow feels like a meeting.

29. Whether it be Glass or a Heart – Asha Jyoti (1984)

Whether it be glass or a heart, it eventually breaks.
No one picks up a broken piece of glass.

I had composed my heart, but it keeps breaking;
I don't even get support for this thing.

Tell the face that this mirror has broken;
The eyes do not find this sight, this scene anymore.

30. The Mischief of the Eyes – Hum Se Hai Zamana (1983)

Is it the mischief of the eyes, or is the heart crazy?
May no one let you go now.

A little love has happened; a little confession has happened.
The matter will move forward; first, let this trust build.
May no one let you go now.

There is a prick in the heartbeat; there is a confusion in the words.
Hold the heart; let the heartbeat become a caravan.
May no one let you go now.

TOP 30 SAD SONGS OF 80s ERA

1. May the String of Life Not Break – Kranti (1981)

May the string of life not break;
Love for a moment or two.
Forget about having a long, long life;
A single moment of love is greater.

What is the laughter of those eyes
In which there is no water [tears]?
That youth is no youth at all
Which has no story to tell.
Tears are the string of happiness.

From today, this is our promise:
We shall meet at every turn.
We shall build a world of the heart,
Leaving behind the fear of the world of sorrow.
Who cares about living or dying?

No matter how deep the ocean is,
Nothing is deeper than love.
Over every wave of the heart's madness,
The heavens do not keep watch.
Every handcuff [restraint] will break.

2. Between You and Me (Sad) – Ek Duuje Ke Liye (1981)

Between you and me, what kind of unknown bond is this?
I didn't know it, you didn't know it.
One end of the thread is pulled, and the other comes running;
It comes bound by a fragile thread, just like someone who is madly in love.

I will wear the bangles from your hands,
My palanquin will go to your courtyard.
No matter what the world does.

Friends speak many languages,
But in this world, love has only one tongue—
The one spoken by the candle and the moth.

3. These Lanes, This Attic – Prem Rog (1982)

These lanes, this attic—never come here again.
Now I have become a stranger [to this place],
For no one of yours is here anymore.

Take away these colorful memories,
The foundations of laughter and crying.
Now I have become a stranger...

My hands are full of bangles; I’ve come to like these green bangles.
Look, your bangles and mine match; my bangles are a friend just like you.
The henna you ground has shown its color; it has stained my fair palms.
Why have your eyes filled with tears, my dear? The wedding flute will play at your house too.

Tell the clouds in the monsoon that my sister is in a foreign land.
We came to meet, and now we leave for our marital home;
We leave only our childhood behind in your courtyard.
Tomorrow too the sun will rise, tomorrow too the birds will sing;
Everyone will be visible to you, but I will not be seen.
Tuck me away in the fold of your veil; forget me in your dreams.

See that you do not forget me, even though I must go far away.
Always keep my innocent mischief nestled in your eyelashes.
When the wedding music begins to play and the world starts to feel empty,
At that moment, just understand that my flower-laden palanquin has departed.
These were bonds of a few days; sometimes we laughed, sometimes we sang.

4. You Who are Smiling So Much – Arth (1982)

You, who are smiling so much—what is the sorrow that you are hiding?
Moisture in the eyes, a smile on the lips—what is your condition? What are you trying to show?

By drinking them over and over, these tears you are drinking will turn into poison.
The wounds that time has begun to heal—why do you keep scratching at them?
Destiny is a game of lines [on the palm]; you are being defeated by those lines.

5. Whether it be Glass or a Heart – Asha Jyoti (1984)

Whether it be glass or a heart, it eventually breaks.
By the time it reaches the lips, the goblet slips from the hands.

Desire alone is not enough; nothing is easy to get.
There is the helplessness of the world, and then destiny is necessary.
These two [heart and world] are such enemies; how can both agree?
If I appease one, the other gets upset.

We were sitting on the shore at the sign of the waves;
We played with storms using the desires of this heart.
We did not know that no one gives support;
The past leaves you, and the shore slips away.

The world is a spectacle; it is hope and despair.
There are a few flowers and some thorns, which destiny has distributed.
Everyone has their own portion, their own story; someone gets plundered, someone gets looted.

6. The Desires of the Heart Flowed Away in Tears – Nikaah (1982)

The desires of the heart flowed away in tears;
Even after being faithful, I remained alone.

Life remained only as a thirst; the stories of love remained incomplete.
Perhaps this is his final cruelty; I endured every cruelty thinking this.
I even erased myself, but the distances that were between us remained.

7. What Do the Conscious Know – Sarfarosh (1999)

What do the conscious know of what ecstasy is?
Fall in love, and then understand what life is.

Since my eyes met hers, the atmosphere has become radiant;
Today I learned what the magic of love is.
Her scattered hair taught poetry to the seasons;
Her bowing eyes explained what intoxication is.

I could never tell her the state of my heart with my lips;
And she did not understand what this silence is.

8. Life is a Song of Love – Souten (1983)

Life is a song of love; every heart will have to sing it.
Life is also an ocean of sorrow; one will have to cross it with a smile.

Life is a feeling; it is some hope for a broken heart.
Life is an exile; everyone will have to serve it and pass through.

What if life is unfaithful? What if our loved ones are upset with us?
What if there is no hand in hand? We will still have to fulfill the companionship.

Life is a smile; it is a recognition of pain.
Life is a guest; one will have to leave the world and go.

9. In Your Eyes, Something... – Ghar (1978)

In your eyes, something—yes, I mean it—makes me want to say: I love you, my beloved.
Your eyes have said something to me—yes, they mean it: you love me, my beloved.

You saw us, we saw you. We saw, and we found.
Having seen, we kept on looking. Having found, we immediately got lost.
I didn't touch you, but since you said yes, it's a "yes."

10. You Are Included in My Life – Aap To Aise Na The (1980)

You are included in my life in such a way, as if darkness is obtained from light.
I never thought I was beautiful in this way; as if you know it, but I didn't.

Not only you, but I too have changed; the era changed in the blink of an eye.
From where do they bring these deals of ruin? If you burn my world, a fragrance will emerge.

11. A Letter Has Arrived – Naam (1986)

A letter has arrived; it has descended from the heavens. The spring of flowers has arrived.
I know my own miracle, my Lord. This letter is no ordinary letter; it is the blessing of my home.

My mother has written to me: "Go, live happily in your world."
"I am fine in that village where there is no rain of happiness."

It is not written that this time our century will come;
But what can I do? It is written that I will miss you.
Ask anyone in my house—what is the matter of happiness today?

12. I Am Not Angry With You, Life – Masoom (1983)

I am not angry with you, life; I am just surprised.
I am contained in your every happiness; I have smiled in your sorrow.
Now, what does it matter to you what I desired or what I found?

Whenever I opened my eyes, I saw you before me, and nothing else.
When you touched me secretly, when you pulled me by the finger...
If not you, then no one else. This heart of mine does not agree.

13. O Beloved – Dil (1990)

O beloved, I cannot live without you. My heart calls out.
The desires of the heart remained in the heart; no matter how much anyone explains, this crazy heart never listens. You at least tell it to stay.

Since our eyes met, what has happened in the heart? I am lost in thought.
The words that were on the lips—why could I not say them?
Why don't you talk of this love through your eyes?

14. I Am Your Enemy – Nagina (1986)

I am your enemy, you are mine. What kind of relationship is this between us?
The moon is in the clouds; this is the bond of love. How can I say it won't listen? Such is this monsoon.
Even after being like me, why do you harbor enmity? You made my fair veil black like musk.

15. I Am Remembering You – Disco Dancer (1982)

I am remembering you; your memory is coming.
My love is yours, your love is mine. I have faith; my heart is restless.
I cannot live any longer after forgetting you. If that moment comes [of forgetting], then I too shall depart.
Your memory comes into my words. Every moment is thirsty; you are settled in my eyes.

16. Promise You Won't Leave – Prem Rog (1982)
Promise you won't leave me; you won't leave my side.
No matter what anyone says, no matter who agrees, consider me yours.
Don't say anything to me now; there is no peace without you.

One day you must come, making me your own. Now I must live here, just for you.
I am here every moment, so why aren't you here too?
There is peace when I am with you; I cannot live here without you.

17. Some of My Belongings – Ijaazat (1987)

Some of my belongings are lying with you.
Some wet nights of the monsoon are kept there, and my promise that she will come.
The matter is simply that I will miss you.

Slowly the fingers shortening the pages, my fingers writing on blank paper...
The chests of the clouds, my fingers descending on wet roads—keep these too.
And those days will surely come; if you say so, I shall sing that song that was found while singing to the stars.

And nothing else—just these marks in the eyes.
The wet eyelashes you had given, and those eyes that kept burning in my memory—keep these too.

18. If You Were Not There – Agar Tum Na Hote (1983)

I wouldn't want to live anymore, if you were not there.
To whom would I go and speak, if you were not there?

Don't you understand this? Even I didn't understand some of it.
We separated with great happiness; there are no such sorrows.
If it weren't for the support of happiness, what would I be?

Sometimes my words gave away the secret of the matter.
I called out from all four directions, but what answer did you give?
Someone would have just come along, if you hadn't come.

19. O Life, Embrace Me – Sadma (1983)

O life, embrace me. I too have held your hands to my heart.
I too have embraced your every sorrow.

How can anyone tell the one who is arriving that without them, even those who were my shadows are no longer there?
They too are no longer there.

20. Life is a New War at Every Step – Meri Jung (1985)

Life is a new war at every step.
How can anyone know when victory belongs to whose heart?
As long as there is breath, let us keep fighting.

Who says that there is no night for the night?
Half the sleep I spent, half someone else did.
Was there no night? Nights are also days.

I asked for everything, but found nothing.
Now if I ask for nothing, still nothing is found.
He gives, but you do not.

21. The Heart is Crazy (Sad) – Maine Pyar Kiya (1989)

The heart is crazy, it won't listen without the beloved.
This is a mad thing, it won't understand by explaining.
It thumps and wanders here and there, day and night.

The world asks for its wishes; I only ask for my beloved.
May my beloved and his home remain safe;
Other than this, the heart asks nothing from God.

22. What Sorrow if I am Alone (Sad) – Qayamat Se Qayamat Tak (1988)

What sorrow is there if I am alone? As long as you have arrived.
What is the sorrow of the world’s grief? As long as you have arrived.

If you are with me, even the darkness is pleasant.
If you are with me, why should I grieve for sorrows?
What is this night? If you are here, every moment is spring.
If you are with me, what business do I have with the world?

23. I Can't Sleep – Dil (1990)

I can't sleep, I find no peace. Why does my heart fear so much to beat?
I saw you for a moment or two, and in those moments, my heart went to you.
Even if I am in my senses, my consciousness still goes only to you.

If the heart burns, let it burn. If the eyes cry, let them cry.
I have become crazy after falling in love; the world considers me mad.

24. A Sigh Emerged – Dil (1990)

A sigh kept emerging from this heart in agony.
I did not know that there was this much pain.

In love, I considered your love to be everything.
Why is your love lost to me?
You used to say that if I found it... having found it, why does this heart still cry?

25. When Something Goes Wrong – Jurm (1990)

When something goes wrong, when a difficulty arises,
You must stay by my side, O companion.

There is no one, nor was there anyone in my life besides you.
When I found you, I found this whole world.

In the dark nights of sorrow, in the shadows of solitude,
When you are standing there, it feels like I’ve forgotten the sorrow of the world.

26. A Vow to Make You Mine (Sad) – Sadak (1991)

I have taken a vow to make you mine. Finding you is the story of my life.

I am crazy for you; do love me.
Hold me in your arms; make me unaware.
My heartbeats have become yours. Tell me, where is the sign of my existence?

27. Chandni, O My Chandni (Emotional) – Chandni (1989)

Chandni, O my Chandni, you are my moonlight.

With the colorful clouds, with the kohl of your eyes,
I have written your name upon this heart.

You did this work without asking; you’ve made me infamous.
Well, leave what people say;
If you love me, then what are you afraid of?
Don't wipe the words written on the heart with your veil.

28. The Day is Wondrous (Emotional) – Qayamat Se Qayamat Tak (1988)

The day is wondrous today—I found you.
Whatever happened, happened; now you and I have met.

Our eyes met, and something happened; why? I don't know why it happened.
Just like that, something happened and my heart beat; who knows why it beat?

29. I Am Remembering You – Love Story (1981)

I am remembering you; you are stealing my sleep.

I remember that season, that wet night.
We met you for the first time together.
Something happened that the heart doesn't know.
What did you do? What does it mean to me? Don't ask this matter.

30. O My Companion – Muqaddar Ka Sikandar (1978)

O my companion, what is life without you?

In the flowers and buds, in the lanes of dreams,
There is nothing without you.

In every heartbeat is the thirst for you; in my breath is your fragrance.
From this earth to that sky, in my sight it is only you.
May this love not break, may you not be upset with me, may this companionship never leave.

Without you my nights are like a hermit, without you my days are like nomads.
My life is like burning drops, all my dreams are extinguished.
Without you mine, without mine yours—this life is no life at all.


TOP 30 ROMANTIC SONGS OF 90s ERA


1. First Intoxication – Jo Jeeta Wohi Sikandar (1992)

First intoxication, first hangover;
New love, new waiting.
What should I do with my condition?
O restless heart...
My restless heart, you tell me.

I feel like flying somewhere in these winds,
Or swinging somewhere in these clouds.
I want to make the sky and the earth one;
Tell me, friends, what should I do and what not?

She spoke in such a way,
She gave me dreams of a thousand colors.
I remained there as if defeated,
And she kissed me with love.

2. Slowly, Slowly – Aashiqui (1990)

Come into my life slowly, slowly;
Steal my heart slowly, slowly.
How much I love you, O beloved,
I have to tell you after meeting you.

The evening is the same, the work is the same, without you, O beloved;
There is no sleep, no peace, without you, O beloved.

Sometimes I sleep, sometimes I cry, without you, O beloved;
Even after gaining everything, I lose it, without you, O beloved.

I’ve settled you in my eyes, hidden you in my heart;
I’ve accepted it—I’ve made you my own.
I decorate dreams, I find only you, without you, O beloved;
Waking through the nights, I call out to you, without you, O beloved.

3. Only You – Bombay (1995)

Only you, only you—how can I live without you?
Come now, come now—don't torment me like this.

O my life, O my life—come dwell in these breaths.
O moon, O moon—come down to the land of my heart.
If there is desire, come and meet me;
Or else do this—unite me with the earth [death].

Look at the madness of these breaths,
That they find no peace.
They said to me: "In your paths,
I shall spread these eyes of mine."
I will give my life from these high mountains
If you don't come anywhere.
You there, my love, if you break my hope,
Then here, I shall leave this world.
I have given life and death into your hands.

(F): I’ve come, I’ve come, look, I have come for you;
I’ve broken every bond for the sake of love.

4. When I Saw You – DDLJ (1995)

When I saw you, I realized, my love,
That love is madness.
Now where shall we go from here?
I shall die in your arms.

My eyes, your dreams;
My heart, your memories.
Oh, what is mine? Everything is yours;
Your life, your breaths.
Tears belonging to you have come into my eyes;
All my sorrows have begun to smile.

This heart doesn't find rest anywhere;
What should I say, what should I do?
Yes, you stay sitting in front of me,
And I shall keep looking at you.
(F): You called out, and look, I have come;
What vow is greater than love?

5. When I Saw a Girl – 1942: A Love Story (1994)

When I saw a girl, it felt like...
Like a blooming rose, like a poet's dream;
Like a bright ray of light, like a deer in the forest;
Like a moonlit night, like a word of softness;
Like a lit lamp in a temple.

When I saw a girl, it felt like...
Like the beauty of the morning, like the winter sun;
Like the tune of a flute, like the life of colors;
Like a swaying vine, like the play of waves;
Like a cool breeze carrying fragrance.

When I saw a girl, it felt like...
Like a dancing peacock, like a silk thread;
Like a song of fairies, like the fire of sandalwood;
Like sixteen adornments, like a shower of nectar;
Like an intoxication that rises slowly, slowly.

6. This is the First Love – Hum Aapke Hain Koun..! (1994)

This is the first love, it is the very first time;
Despite knowing, it is unknown—what kind of love is mine?

Her gaze looks at me through the screen of her eyelashes;
Her shyness reveals the secret of her own desire.
The one who is faithful in secret—such is my beloved.

She is the night (Nisha), she is the dawn of my life;
She knows—the thread of my life is in her hands.
Unique from the whole world—such is my love.

7. Gambler, O Gambler – Baazigar (1993)

(F): Oh, my heart was alone, you played such a game;
I stay awake all night in your memory.
Gambler, O Gambler, you are a great magician.

(M): Gambler, I am a Gambler, the beloved of those with hearts;
Oh, I took a heart and gave a heart; I’ve made a deal of love.
I won the game of the heart by losing my heart.

(F): Secretly, through the path of the eyes, you entered my heart;
Awakening the magic of desire, you made me crazy.
(M): In the first sight, you became the queen of my dreams;
This world will remember the story of our faithfulness.
(F): Oh, stealing my peace, flying away with my sleep,
Do not get lost at some turn.

(M): This heart beats "dhak dhak"—tell me, what is it saying?
(F): Come close and I’ll tell you... no, I am feeling scared.
(M): Do not misunderstand me, I am not a wandering cloud;
On the walls of my heart, I have written your name.
Oh, I sacrifice my heart and life for your love;
May you receive my lifespan.

8. My Heart is So Crazy – Saajan (1991)

My heart is so crazy too,
That it loves you so much.
But when you come in front of me,
It is afraid to say anything.

O my beloved, my beloved.
How much I try to make it understand,
How much I try to coax it;
It is innocent, it understands nothing;
Day and night, it only heaves sighs.

Every moment it torments me,
It keeps me awake all night.
You have no news of this—
That it beats only for you.

9. Water Fell, Pitter-Patter – Mohra (1994)

The water fell pitter-patter;
The water set a fire.
When the fire started in the heart,
The heart remembered you.
When I remembered you, my drenched body
Began to burn.
Now you tell me, beloved, what should I do?

Your name had come to my lips;
Oh, I called you on an excuse.
The monsoon has arrived swaying—what should I do?

Drown in the river, while I stand on the shore;
You fell upon my heart like a bolt of lightning.
Such a crazy wind has blown—what should I do?

When I remembered you, madness overcame me;
My mind is not in my control—what should I do?

10. The King Fell in Love with the Queen – Akele Hum Akele Tum (1995)

The King fell in love with the Queen;
In the first sight, the first love happened.
Both heart and soul were wounded;
The arrow of the gaze went through the heart.

Oh, path to path, arm to arm—
Even after meeting, they do not meet.
Oh, it often happens that the buds of desire
Do not bloom even after blooming.
Yet, who knows why it doesn't listen?
The crazy heart has become restless.

Look at the Queen—when our eyes met,
She began to steal her gaze.
Oh, what else could she do? She bowed her head
And began to turn her bangle.
Oh, the King worked such magic,
That despite saying "no," the confession happened.

11. I Have Given My Heart, Beloved – Hum Dil De Chuke Sanam (1999)

I have given my heart, beloved; distances are not acceptable.
Where you are and where I am—it is impossible to say.
I have given my heart, beloved... Come to me.

What kind of dreams I have given to you;
And how many dreams there are that haven't left yet.
I was with you when these lamps were burning;
Who knows why they, too, burned away in helplessness.

12. Having Stolen My Heart – Main Khiladi Tu Anari (1994)

Having stolen my heart, smiling stealthily,
You departed, my love, after looting me.
If we meet alone, how shall I say
The things that have been in my heart for many days?

In our meeting, you met me with such love;
You met me with such trust of affection.
What has happened? Why has it happened?
Tell me, how does this love happen?

I tell you truly, my love,
I have tried loving you from my heart.
It was very difficult to meet in love;
If you wish, I can tell you [how].

13. Something Happens – Kuch Kuch Hota Hai (1998)

Something happens, something happens...
You don't know, I don't know, but something happens.

What can I say, why is the weather like this?
That there is less sleep at night now.
After meeting you, something happens like this...
You don't know, I don't know.

I was in love, and so were you.
When someone falls for someone else,
Something happens.

14. The Moon Hid in the Clouds – Hum Dil De Chuke Sanam (1999)

The moon hid in the clouds, its head bowed in shame.
Looking at the moon, one day I had thought...

Silence on the lips, intoxication in the eyes;
In the heartbeat today, there is a special sign.
Tell me a little, let me know a little:
What is this weather saying?
Let no one ask me what happened today.

The beloved says this is the moment of love;
Enjoy this moment, for we may never meet like this again.
Come into my arms, my love, you alone are my companion.
What is this heart saying?

15. The Heart is Crazy – Dil To Pagal Hai (1997)

The heart is crazy, the heart is mad.
Why it beats, it doesn't even know.

Whose face is this in my eyes?
Why does it come into my dreams?
He is hidden behind the curtains of the eyelashes;
He is saying: the heart is mad.

It is the season of love, there is a cool breeze.
No one is here, yet there is happiness.
And I don't have anyone's company;
Then why do I have this complaint?
The heart is mad.

16. Match the Rhythm to the Rhythm – Taal (1999)

Match the rhythm to the rhythm, match the note to the note.
You and I have met, we have met in such a way...
May this meeting never create a separation.

If hearts met, did peace come or not?
Was love found or not? Was the goblet found or not?
I only know this—by joining heart to heart,
Whatever was found, was found by losing [oneself].

Why does it beat "dhak dhak," why is it afraid?
There is intoxication in the eyes, who knows why.
What kind of fire is this in the heartbeat?
The night is wide awake, what melody is this?
The atmosphere of love has spread;
This crazy mind does not listen.

17. The Mischief of the Eyes – Hum Dil De Chuke Sanam (1999)

These are the mischiefs of the eyes, for the heart is crazy.
Let no one let you go now.

A little love has happened, a little confession has happened;
The matter will move forward, but first let this trust happen.

There is a pang in the heartbeat, a confusion in the words;
Hold onto the heart, let the caravan of the heartbeat begin.

If not today, then tomorrow; if not tomorrow, then the day after;
I must meet you—let that day happen somewhere.

18. O Stranger – Dil Se.. (1998)

O stranger, you too, sometime, call out from somewhere.

If you say so, I'll take these distances to the sky;

19. The Heart Does Not Agree – Dil Hai Ke Manta Nahin (1991)

It's a heart that doesn't agree; they are eyes that don't stop.

As if we are not ourselves, even though we are here.

The way you looked at me with a smile...

20. Breath is Necessary – Aashiqui (1990)

21. Steal Me From Myself – Mohabbatein (2000)

22. I Shall Sing Such a Song – Yes Boss (1997)

23. Neither the Stroke of Kohl – Mohra (1994)

25. This Heart is Crazy – Pardes (1997)

30. Say That it is Love – Kaho Naa... Pyaar Hai (2000)

TOP 30 SAD SONGS OF 90s ERA


1. Suffering, Suffering – Hum Dil De Chuke Sanam (1999)

Your love awakened this lifeless heart,
And then your love itself ruined this heart.
Suffering, suffering, sighs kept rising from this heart;
You punished me for loving, what crime had I committed?
I’ve been robbed, yes, I’ve been robbed in your love.

Love is strange, my friend—moments of happiness,
But you find treasures of sorrow and loneliness.
Sometimes tears, sometimes sighs, sometimes complaints, sometimes wails.
I see your face in the light of day,
Your memories torment me in the darkness of night.

If I meet God, I will ask:
"After giving me a body of clay, why did You make a heart of glass?
And then gave it a nature that falls in love?"
How strange is Your nature—on top of that, You gave me a fate
Of sometimes meeting, and sometimes separation.

2. How Can I Live – Saajan (1991)

How can I live without you?
The heart finds no rest anywhere without you.
How can I say what life will be like without you?
It will be like a punishment, like a curse.
I have made this decision: I don't want to live without you.

If someone gives me poison, I will drink it with a smile;
I will bear every pain, I will live in any condition.
But I won't be able to bear the pain of separation;
I won't be able to stay without you.

Your lowering of the eyelashes upon seeing me,
And your smile—I remember them so much.
How can I forget all those words, those sweet nights, and those meetings?

3. The Heart Does Not Agree (Sad) – Dil Hai Ke Manta Nahin (1991)

The heart is such that it doesn't agree;
The ritual of love is very difficult, it just doesn't understand.
Why this restlessness is happening, it just doesn't know.

The heart wants that every moment, I should just keep looking at you.
Even in death, let us not be separated—let's do something like that.
Merge into me, come close to me, my companion, my soulmate.

4. I Have Loved You – Mann (1999)

I have loved you and will love you every moment;
Even after death, this love will not diminish from the heart.

When your memory comes, my tears flow.
"Our union will happen"—my heart says this every moment.
What kind of life is this? It is just your story.

Those words of yours, those rituals of love...
Were the promises false? Were the vows lies?
O desire of my soul, is this true? Just tell me this much.
Let the moment of trust not break; give some reward for my waiting.

You are in front of me, yet why is there this distance?
How can I tell you—alas, what helplessness this is!
Is this any way to live? Just to drink one's own tears?

5. We Are Here – Zakhm (1998)

We are here, you are here, the heart is young—
But where is that old spark?

TOP 30 TELUGU SAD SONGS

1. I asked, I asked

Chorus:
I asked, I asked the rhythm in my heart,
Oh, passing moment! Where is my love?
I forgot myself, thinking only of her name,
I asked my heart where her essence is.

Verse 1:
I can't imagine myself without you.
When I search in every thought of mine, I only find our story.
I am within you, desiring you,
I walked with you as if it was my destiny, together.

Verse 2:
You are in the depths of my heart.
You are my other half and my entire world.
Your friendship is the tune that guides me.
This journey doesn't stop at just reaching you;
It's a journey with no fatigue.

2. My Dearest, My Dearest

Chorus:

If you are not with me, my world falls apart.

I search for you and only you.
The journey that began with you,
Should it end this way?

I will become the sky and embrace you.

8. The Wealth of Moonlight

Chorus:
The moon king and the earth queen,
They were united, weren't they, by the sacred moonlight (Sirivennela)?
Is it distance? Is it distance?
Come to me, become my shore, in the middle of this night.

Verse 1:
It opened the curtains in my sleep,
Did it wake up, forgetting its drowsiness?
The charming, overflowing beauty, the first dream.
In her smiles...
The sparkle, the sparkle on her cheeks,
The flush, the flush of her anklets,
A new dream, refreshing again and again.

Pre-Chorus:
Oh, my love, is this how my wealth feels?
Said this serene night.
Glimmering, glimmering in my beloved's eyes,
Having seen her dreams,
She blossoms with wonder.
Oh my, what a beautiful woman!
There is surely no one to match her.
Was she born when creation looked into a mirror?
This delicate, beautiful woman.
This is a meeting that has occurred between the sky and the earth.

Verse 2:
The maiden who is seeing the light,
Beyond the screen, beyond the boundaries.
The time that greets me,
Stirring the rival within my heart.
Watching with love,
The night tingles with goosebumps.
Mixing words, blooming as hopes,
The dawn lines that blossom in the nine-night (Navaratri) season.
Written like a novel.
Silences become affections,
Become sweet poems,
Like a message that never ends.
The story moves on, like a preface to tomorrow's tales.
In her smiles...
The sparkle, the sparkle on her cheeks,
The flush, the flush of her anklets,
A new dream, refreshing again and again.

Verse 3:
No one has ever shown it to my eyes, saying "it's like this."
She herself doesn't know how to put it into words.
Even if the heart is won just now,
The sight is only found tomorrow.
Even if happiness sparkles alongside hesitation,
Can we deny the ecstatic joys that surpass those two?
As the story progresses,
As their hearts get wet,
The thirst hidden throughout past births,
Is it just now being introduced to them?
In her smiles...
The sparkle, the sparkle on her cheeks,
The flush, the flush of her anklets,
A new dream, refreshing again and again.
In her smiles...
The sparkle, the sparkle on her cheeks,
The flush, the flush of her anklets,
A new dream, refreshing again and again.

9. Oh Sita, Oh Rama

Verse 1:
Oh Sita, I will be with you, never leaving you.
All day long, I will be your shadow, giving you light.
I will be the lines on your palm, guiding your way,
I will move forward, never letting go of your hand.

Destiny has revealed the shore, the lines on my forehead,
I will settle upon you like a sacred mark (tilakam).
I will become the dream that dances like lightning in your eyes.

Chorus:
Oh Rama, shall we become each other's?
Shall we walk together in step with time?
Can we write what will happen tomorrow?
Become like the pen that writes it.

Can we paint an entire lifetime together as a pair?
Show me the brush that paints it.
Hidden in the lightning, in the thunder,
Is the truth, please see it, my dear.

Verse 2:
Oh Sita, I will be with you, never leaving you.
Oh Rama, shall we become each other's?

All the words that cannot be spoken directly upwards,
Are your glances, falling to the ground,
Becoming flowers with every step.

On one side, an unbearable intoxication pulls me,
On the other side, a world.
In this time of confusion,
In this heart that can't decide anything,
Is it shyness? Is it a rule?
What is the name of this chain that holds me back?

Bridge:
Sleep will not disturb me, I will only murmur your name.
Knowing that this is a service for a full hundred years, you still delay.

There is a strange pain that never existed before,
The moment for that pain to sing has arrived.
Will you come along, Oh Time?
Blend dreams like slumber.
So that the service can be with the servant,
Show me your magic, my dear.

Chorus:
Oh Rama, shall we become each other's?
Shall we walk together in step with time?
I will be the lines on your palm, guiding your way,
I will move forward, never letting go of your hand.

Destiny has revealed the shore, the lines on my forehead,
I will settle upon you like a sacred mark (tilakam).
I will become the dream that dances like lightning in your eyes.

Oh, Moonlight of Love,

Verse 1:

Like a new flower that has just bloomed.

Like a new color that has slipped down to the earth.

Pre-Chorus:

Like the Krishna river swelling in the heart.

Oh, you who have made the musical notes (sa ri ga ma) so sweet.

Pallavi (Chorus):
Oh, Moonlight of Love,
Please come, Urmila.

Verse 2:
Hmm... When you apply kohl to your eyes like that,
Won't the day turn into midnight?

My heart gets disturbed, filled with restless feelings.

Pre-Chorus:
Like jewels that have vanished from the king's ornaments,
Like a single star coming down to earth,
Have you transformed into those two eyes of yours?
The poet Vemana said, "One true sapphire is enough,"
Looking at you, he must have written that.

Pallavi (Chorus):
Oh, Moonlight of Love,
Please come, Urmila.

Verse 3:
Oh, beautiful one, don't walk like that in my dreams.
If your foot gets hurt, my heart will writhe in pain.
Don't speak in such a flow, your words like a stream.
If your lips tremble, my heart will weep.

Pre-Chorus:

Verse 1 (Male):
Even when I think of you, your name is what I speak,

With my silence,
I have captured your heart.
Forgive me, my love.

Chorus (Male):

Here, I declare that this life is yours.

Verse 2 (Female):

The first time I heard the thrilling nuances in your words,
It's enough, it's enough, my love.
In this lifetime, I have heard your calls.

Chorus (Female):

Bridge (Both):

Verse 2:

27. " Without You, Shyam (DJ Mix)" – Hemant Chauhan

Chorus:
Without you, Shyam, without you, Shyam,
My heart feels so alone.
Without you, Shyam, without you, Shyam,
I'm like a bird with no home.

Verse 1:
The beat drops and I lose control,
Body and soul.
I dance with fire in my feet,
So sweet.

Verse 2:
The lights flash colors all around,
On sacred ground.
I feel your presence in the bass,
In this place.

Verse 3:
Old words with a modern sound,
All around.
Devotion meets the dance floor tonight,
In the light.

Verse 4:
The young and old come together,
In any weather.
United by your holy name,
Burning in flame.

Bridge:
Without you, Shyam, without you, Shyam,
My heart feels so alone.
Even with the beat so strong,
To you I belong.

Chorus:
Without you, Shyam, without you, Shyam,
My heart feels so alone.
Without you, Shyam, without you, Shyam,
I'm like a bird with no home.

Chorus:

So sweet.

On this floor.

When we call.

Everywhere.

Verse 4:

Across this land.

Come what may.

Bridge:

On this floor.

30. " Oh Drummer, Play the Drum " – Kirtidan Gadhvi

Chorus:
Oh drummer, play the drum, play the drum,
Let the rhythm come.
Oh drummer, play the drum, play the drum,
Make us overcome.

Verse 1:
The beat starts slow, then builds up fast,
Long may it last.
It calls the dancers to the floor,
Wanting more.

Verse 2:
The circles form and hands go up,
Like a cup.
The claps echo through the night,
So bright.

Verse 3:
I lose myself in every sound,
All around.
My feet can't help but move along,
To the song.

Verse 4:
This music brings us all as one,
Beneath the sun.
It celebrates our joy and love,
From above.

Bridge:
Oh drummer, play the drum, play the drum,
Let the rhythm come.
Play for us all night long,
With your song.

Chorus:
Oh drummer, play the drum, play the drum,
Let the rhythm come.
Oh drummer, play the drum, play the drum,
Make us overcome.
